Gradient computation via FFT:
```
def gradient_from_weights(W, a, N):
    # W length 2n-1 (float)
    Aw=fft(W,N)
    A=fft(a,N)
    y=ifft(Aw * np.conj(A))
    return 2.0 * np.real(y[:n])
```

Mirror update details:
```
def mirror_step(a, grad, eta):
    gm = grad.max()
    if gm <= 0: return a
    r = grad/gm
    # subtract mean to preserve
    r0 = r - r.mean()
    # limit exponent to avoid underflow
    max_exp = min(10, eta * np.abs(r0).max())? If eta*r0 large, clip.
    logfac = np.clip(eta * r0, -10, 10)
    b = a * np.exp(-logfac)
    b = np.maximum(b,0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return a
    return b/s
```

Let's implement `optimize_array(a, n, time_slice, rng, target=1.50, use_mirror=True)` that can be reused. This is core.

Detailed core local optimizer design:
Inputs: a length n nonnegative finite, time_limit seconds, rng, target optional.
Normalization: a = np.clip(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12 return uniform; a = a/s (sum1).
Precompute N = 1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
Best = a.copy(); best_score = score a.
Parameters:
- beta/p schedule? Use `p` from 8 to 1? We'll alternate.
- mirror eta. Use normalization of grad max.
- Maybe use `cmax` to compute target.
- Use `lbfgs`? No.

Pseudo:
```
def local_optimize(a, n, time_limit, rng, target=1.502):
    start=time.monotonic()
    a=normalize(a,n)
    N=...
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
    eta=0.25
    p=16
    p_dir=1
    no_improve=0
    iteration=0
    hard_limit=time_limit + 30
    while time < hard_limit:
        c=convolve(a,N)
        cur=2*n*c.max()
        if cur < best_sc: best_sc=cur; best=a.copy()
        if best_sc <= target*0.99: break
        # choose weights
        q = c / cmax if cmax>0
        # Can use threshold: q[q < 0.8] = 0? maybe for high p no need.
        W = q.astype(float)**p
        # if sum W too small? p high; normalize
        Wsum=W.sum()
        if Wsum <=0: W = c.copy(); Wsum = W.sum()
        W /= Wsum
        grad = grad_weights(W, a, N)
        gm=grad.max()
        if gm <=0: break
        g=grad/gm
        # Try mirror step
        for trial in 0..1:
           if trial==0: new=mirror(a,g,eta)
           else: new=mirror(a,g,eta/3)
           cnew=convolve(new,N); scnew=2*n*cnew.max()
           if scnew < cur*(1-1e-10):
               a=new; cur=scnew; no_improve=0; improve...
               if scnew>?? break
               break
        else:
           # try Euclidean projected step? or no improvement
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 30:
              # perturb: multiplicative noise
              a = normalize(a * exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n)), n)
              # reset eta, p
              eta = rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)
              p = rng.choice([4,8,16,32])
              no_improve=0
              continue
        # adapt p/eta
        if p < 4: p_dir=1
        elif p > 32: p_dir=-1
        p*= (1+0.05*p_dir) maybe
        if cur < prev? no
    return best,best_sc
```

Let's design a population loop for primary n:
```
def run_population(n, starts, budget, rng, target):
    candidates = starts normalized
    polish each candidate briefly in round-robin to select.
    best_pool = [top K by score]
    global best
    time while:
       pick candidate with lowest score or randomly among top
       run local_optimize for slice (e.g. 5s)
       if improved, update
       every 30s create new candidate by mutation of best: add random Fourier noise or interpolate with uniform, quick polish 5s, replace worst if better.
```
But local_optimize itself long; easier allocate large time to best candidate and alternate with secondary.

Maybe use "simulated annealing" at shape level: For candidate a, take b from pool, mix `m = (a+b)/2`, normalize, local optimize. This can combine good shapes.

Let's implement simpler deterministic sequence:
1. Prepare starts for n=8192 (and maybe n=16384). For each start, do a quick `local_optimize` with small time (2-5s) to assess. Track global best.
2. Use remaining time, repeatedly:
   - Choose best candidate and run local_optimize for a time chunk (say 20-60s) until target or no improvement.
   - After each chunk, generate 1-2 mutants from best (random Fourier noise, mix with second best, resample from n_small?), quick local_optimize 10s, add if better.
   - Move to next candidate if best stuck.
3. At final, run final polish on global best for remaining time.

Need ensure not too many function calls with time slices causing overhead. local_optimize should accept time_limit seconds.

def normalize_sum(a, n):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   if len!=n: interpolate? or return generic

   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300: a=np.ones(n)/n

`full_convol(arr, N)`:
```

Gradient:
```
def grad_weights(W, a, N):
    FW = fft(W, N)
    Fa = fft(a, N)
    y = ifft(FW * np.conj(Fa))
    return 2.0 * y.real[:n]
```
Need length W = 2n-1. If W normalized sum1, gradient max ~? For uniform, W central p~1/n, grad~2*1/n ~2.4e-4. Good.

Mirror step:
```
def mirror_update(a, g, eta):
    gm = g.max()
    if gm <= 1e-300: return a
    r = g / gm
    r0 = r - r.mean()
    # Clip exponent
    e = eta * r0
    # For stability, if eta large, scale e to max 8
    emax = np.max(np.abs(e))
    if emax > 8.0:
        e *= 8.0/emax
    b = a * np.exp(-e)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = b.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       return a
    return b / s
```
Why not subtract average in exponent? Exponent mean not exactly zero; normalization handles. But if e has large negative, zeros. Clip.
Could also use multiplicative ratio: `b = a * np.exp(-eta*(r-r_min))`; normalization.

Euclidean step:
```
def euclidean_update(a, g, step):
    # g normalized? use unit gradient? Use g/gnorm
    norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
    if norm==0: return a
    d = -g/norm
    x = a + step*d
    # project simplex sum1
    return project_simplex(x, 1.0)
```
Step could be 0.05/norm_a? Norm_a ~1/√n. Use step=0.01? Since d unit, x change 0.01 (much larger than mean 1/n? 0.01*? For n=8192 mean 1e-4, step 0.01 changes entire vector l2 by 0.01, significant. Projection handles. But direction from gradient may be good. Use small 0.001.
Maybe not needed.

Pseudo local:
```
def local_optimize(initial, n, time_limit, rng, target=1.5027):
    a=normalize; N=...
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=score
    c=full_convol; cur=score
    eta=0.2; p=16; use_thresh=False; noacc=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    while time < deadline:
       # optionally every 200 iters recompute c for a? We have c.
       if cur < best_sc: update
       if best_sc <= target*0.999: break
       # time check
       # select weights
       q = c / cmax
       if use_thresh:
          gap = 0.1*cmax  # maybe adaptive
          mask = q > (1-gap/cmax?) actually q 0..1; thresh q> 0.85
          W = np.zeros_like(c); W[mask]=q[mask]**p; s=W.sum(); if s<1: W=c.copy(); s=c.sum()
       else:
          W = q**p
       Wn = W / W.sum()
       g=grad_weights(Wn,a,N)
       gm=g.max()
       if gm <=0: break
       gn=g/gm
       improved=False
       # trial eta
       e_try = eta
       new=mirror_update(a,gn,e_try)
       cn=full_convol(new,N); sc=2*n*cn.max()
       if sc < cur*(1-1e-11):
          a=new; c=cn; cur=sc; noacc=0; improved=True; eta=min(eta*1.1,2.0); noacc=0
       else:
          # smaller
          e_try = eta*0.25
          new2=mirror_update(a,gn,e_try)
          cn2=full_convol(new2,N); sc2=2*n*cn2.max()
          if sc2 < cur*(1-1e-11):
             a=new2; c=cn2; cur=sc2; noacc=0; eta=e_try; improved=True
          else:
             noacc +=1
             if noacc % 3 ==0:
                eta=max(eta*0.8,0.01)
                p = switch p
                use_thresh = not use_thresh every 5
             if noacc > 50:
                # perturb
                a = perturb(a,n,rng)
                c=full_convol(a,N); cur=2*n*c.max()
                eta=0.2; p=16; noacc=0
       # if improved many, reduce p to flatten? Maybe
       if improved and p > 2: p = max(2,p*0.99)
       # if stuck, random p
    return best,best_sc
```

def theta_score(theta, n):
    a = fourier_ab_sequence(theta, n)
    if a is None: return 1e9
    # local score with N small
    N = 1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()
    c = full_convol(a, N)
    return 2.0*n*c.max()

Can use it as "spectral correction" with c_des flattened:
```
def conv_spectral_update(a,N, gamma, time...):
  c=full_convol(a,N); cmax
  q=c/cmax
  c_des = cmax * (q**gamma)  # q<=1, gamma<1 flattens
  # Maybe keep sum? c_des_sum = q^gamma sum; Anew scale changes.
  A=fft(a,N)
  C=A*A
  ratio=np.ones_like(C)
  mask=np.abs(C)>1e-30
  ratio[mask]=fft(c_des,N)[mask] / C[mask]
  # ratio may have large magnitude; clip magnitude to [0.1,10]
  mag=np.abs(ratio); scaled=mag.clip(0.1,10); ratio *= scaled/mag where mag>0
  Anew=A*np.sqrt(ratio)
  a_new=real(ifft(Anew))[:n]
  a_new=project_simplex(a_new,1.0)
  return a_new, score
```

def local_optimize(start, n, time_limit, rng, target=1.50):
    a=normalize; N=...
    best=a; best_sc=score
    c=full_convol; cur=score
    eta=0.15; p=12; use_thresh=False; noacc=0; it=0
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+time_limit+5? (within outer limits too)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       if cur < best: update
       if best <= target: break
       # main mirror
       cmax=c.max(); q=c/cmax
       if use_thresh and cmax>0:
          thresh=max(1e-30, cmax - 0.1*cmax)?? Actually threshold in c: c > cmax*(1-gap)
          W = np.zeros_like(c); mask=q > 0.8?; W[mask]=q[mask]**p; if W.sum()<1: W=q**p
       else: W = q**p
       Wn=W/W.sum()
       g=grad_weights(Wn,a,N)
       # try mirror
       for factor in [1.0,0.3]:
          new=mirror_update(a,g/factor?) # g normalized inside
          new_sc, new_c = eval_candidate(new,N)
          if new_sc < cur*(1-1e-10): accept; break
       else:
          # try spectral flatten
          if it % 3 == 0:
             best_trial=None
             for gamma in (0.5,0.3):
                 trial, sc2 = spectral_trial(a,c,N,gamma)
                 if sc2 < cur: accept
             if no: noacc +=1; adapt; if noacc>30 perturb
       # adapt p
       p = ...
    return best,best_sc
```

Perturbation:
```
def perturb_array(a,n,rng,mode):
   if mode==0: # log noise
      factor=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3,n))
      b=a*factor
   elif mode==1: # add mode
      m=rng.integers(1,7)
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      amp=(1.0/n)*rng.uniform(0.05,0.5)
      x=np.arange(n)
      b=a + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x/n + phase)
      b=np.maximum(b,0)
   else: # anti-palindromic mode
      m... amp...
      b=a + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*(x+0.5)/n)
   b=b-b.min()+? if negative; normalize
```
If b sum <=0, return a.

Maybe include "palindromic mode" to adjust? We want anti-symmetric for central. Use sine modes.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0):
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic(); budget=float(budget_s if budget_s else 2340)
    deadline=t0+min(budget,2380)-10
    global_best=np.inf; global_a=None
    prev=get_previous()
    # If prev exists, primary n = min(max(len(prev),4096),16384)? If prev length 8192, n=8192.
    n1=8192
    # Build initial starts n1:
    starts=[]
    if prev is not None:
       if len(prev)==n1: starts.append(normalize(prev))
       else: starts.append(interp(prev,n1))
    #-fourier optimized small
    small_best = small_shape_search(... maybe budget 120)
    for small in small_best: starts.append(interp(small,n1))
    # Add random Fourier direct n1 (6)
    for i in range(8): starts.append(fourier_ab_sequence(random_ab(), n1))
    # maybe previous spectral flatten? no.
    # Deduplicate? not necessary.
    # For each start, run local_optimize with initial_chunk 15s (or 10 depending count)
    pool=[]
    for idx,start in enumerate(starts):
       if time left <30: break
       chunk = min(15, remaining / max(1,len(starts)-idx))
       if chunk <2: break
       arr,sc=local_optimize(start,n1,chunk,rng,target=1.5)
       if finite: pool.append([arr,sc]); update global
    # sort pool by sc; keep top 8
    # iterative improve
    while time left > 120 and pool not empty:
       pool.sort(key=score)
       # Choose chunk based on remaining and pool size: min(45, remaining*0.2)
       # Improve top 2 or 3 round-robin
       chunk = min(30, max(5, (deadline-now)*0.1))
       for k in range(min(3,len(pool))):
           a,sc=local_optimize(pool[k][0], n1, chunk, rng)
           pool[k]=[a,sc]; update global
           if k==0 and (now %?) generate mutant:
              mut=perturb_array(a,n1,rng,rng.integers(0,3))
              msc=local_optimize(mut,n1,5)
              if msc < pool[-1][1]: replace or append (limit 8)
       # if global best improved, maybe make it first
    # n2=16384 if remaining > 300:
    n2=16384
    arr2=interp(global_a,n2) if finite else ...; maybe also interp top small; add previous if same.
    starts2 = unique arr2, perturb
    local for start chunks; update global
    final polish global best on its length for remaining (or try smaller if too high? no)
```

`random_ab(rng, M, scale)`:
```
arr=np.zeros(2*M)
for m in range(1,M+1):
   amp = rng.uniform(0.1,1.0) * (1.0/(m**0.2)) * scale
   phase = rng.uniform(0,2π)
   arr[2*m-1] = amp*np.cos(phase)  # A
   arr[2*m] = amp*np.sin(phase)    # B
```
For anti sine, phase=π/2. Fixed starts: M=6, amps [0.9,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.15,0.1] phase π/2 (sin odd/even all anti). Also random phases.

u=np.zeros(n) if n>0

for k in range(m):
   A=theta[2*k]; B=theta[2*k+1]
   if A!=0 or B!=0:

      u += A*np.cos(ang) + B*np.sin(ang)
f=1.0+u
fmin=f.min()
if fmin < 0:
   scale = 0.98 / max(1e-12, -fmin)
   f = 1.0 + u*scale
# if max positive huge, not issue
f=np.maximum(f,0.0)
s=f.sum()
if s<1e-30: return np.ones(n)/n
return f/s
```

def optimize_pool(starts, n, budget_s, rng, target=1.50, max_pool=6, initial_chunk=15.0):
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(0.0,budget_s)
   pool=[]
   # initial assessments
   for idx, arr in enumerate(starts):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20: break
      if initial_chunk is None:
         chunk = max(3.0, min(30.0, (deadline-time.monotonic()) / max(1,(len(starts)-idx))))
      else: chunk=initial_chunk
      if chunk<2: break
      a,sc=local_optimize(arr,n,chunk,rng,target)
      if finite: pool.append((sc,a))
   # sort keep

Let's write `local_optimize` in detail.

```
def local_optimize(initial, n, time_limit, rng, target=1.50):
    a = normalize_seq(initial, n)
    if n <= 0: return a, np.inf
    N = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    A = fft(a, N)
    C = A*A
    c = np.real(ifft(C))[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    cur = 2.0*n*c.max()/ (s*s) # but normalized so s=1. Use cmax*2n.
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur
    eta = 0.2
    p = 12.0
    use_thresh = False
    no_acc = 0
    it = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.5, time_limit)
    # also respect global? local doesn't know. okay.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if cur < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur; best_a = a.copy()
        if best_sc <= target:
            break
        cmax = c.max()
        if cmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        q = c / cmax
        # weights
        if use_thresh:
            mask = q > 0.75
            W = np.zeros_like(c)
            W[mask] = q[mask] ** p
            if W.sum() < 1e-12:
                W = q ** p
        else:
            W = q ** p
        Wsum = W.sum()
        if Wsum <= 0:
            W = np.ones_like(c)/c.size
        else:
            W = W / Wsum
        FW = fft(W, N)
        g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(FW * np.conj(A)))[:n]
        gm = g.max()
        accepted = False
        if gm > 1e-300:
            gn = g / gm
            for factor in (1.0, 0.25):
                new_a = mirror_update(a, gn, eta * factor)
                newA = fft(new_a, N)
                newc = np.real(ifft(newA * newA))[:2*n-1]
                newc = np.maximum(newc, 0.0)
                sc = 2.0*n*newc.max()
                if sc < cur * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                    a = new_a; A = newA; c = newc; cur = sc
                    no_acc = 0
                    eta = min(2.0, eta * 1.05)
                    accepted = True
                    # update best if better
                    break
            # if not accepted, try smaller static maybe? enough
        if not accepted:
            # spectral updates
            tried = 0
            if (it % 3 == 0) or no_acc > 8:
                gamma_list = (0.6, 0.35)
                red_list = (0.05, 0.15)
                best_trial = None; best_trial_sc = cur
                for gamma in gamma_list:
                    for red in red_list:
                        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                            break
                        trial_a, trial_sc = spectral_update(a, A, c, N, gamma, red, 0.0)
                        if trial_sc < best_trial_sc:
                            best_trial_sc = trial_sc
                            best_trial = trial_a
                if best_trial is not None and best_trial_sc < cur * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                    a = best_trial; cur = best_trial_sc
                    A = fft(a, N)
                    c = np.maximum(np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1],0.0)
                    no_acc = 0
                    accepted = True
                    eta = max(0.05, eta*0.8)
            if not accepted:
                no_acc += 1
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.85)
                # adapt p
                if p > 3.0:
                    p *= 0.95
                elif p < 30.0:
                    p *= 1.05
                use_thresh = not use_thresh
                if no_acc > 30:
                    mode = rng.integers(0,3)
                    a = perturb_array(a, n, rng, mode)
                    A = fft(a,N)
                    c = np.maximum(np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1],0.0)
                    cur = 2.0*n*c.max()
                    eta = 0.2
                    p = 12.0
                    use_thresh = False
                    no_acc = 0
                # If current score much worse than best, maybe reset to best to focus?
                if cur > best_sc * 1.2 and no_acc > 60:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    A = fft(a,N)
                    c = ...
                    cur = best_sc
                    eta=0.1; p=20; no_acc=0
        # if accepted many times, slowly reduce p to flatten
        if accepted and p > 2.0:
            p *= 0.999
```
Some inefficiencies: If `accepted` true, at next loop recompute A,c. Good. If spectral accepted, update A,c. If mirror accepted inside factor loop, continues skip spectral.

Spectral_update current code uses A and c, but after mirror we have A. Good.
```
def spectral_update(a, A, c, N, gamma, reduction, floor):
    n=len(a)
    cmax=c.max()
    target=cmax*(1.0-reduction)
    q=c/cmax
    if floor>0: q=np.maximum(q,floor)
    c_des = target * np.power(q, gamma)
    # ensure length correct
    Cdes = fft(c_des, N)
    C = A*A
    ratio = np.ones_like(C, dtype=np.complex128)
    absC = np.abs(C)
    # If A*C maybe zero?
    mask = absC > (1e-25 * (absC.max() + 1e-300))
    ratio[mask] = Cdes[mask] / C[mask]
    # Clip magnitude
    mag = np.abs(ratio)
    # For zero mag? if mask false ratio1
    over = mag > 10.0
    if np.any(over): ratio[over] *= 10.0 / mag[over]
    under = (mag > 0.0) & (mag < 0.1)
    if np.any(under): ratio[under] *= 0.1 / mag[under]
    Anew = A * np.sqrt(ratio)
    b = np.real(ifft(Anew))[:n]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = b.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return a, 2.0*n*cmax #? But c max old score? Use score_seq.
    b /= s
    # compute score
    B = fft(b,N)
    cb = np.maximum(np.real(ifft(B*B))[:2*n-1],0.0)
    sc = 2.0*n*cb.max()
    return b, sc

```
def project_simplex(v,S):
   v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   n=v.size
   if n==0: return v
   v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
   v=np.maximum(v,0.0)
   if S <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
   # if sum close S, return
   cs = v.sum()
   if cs < 1e-300:
       return np.full(n, S/n)
   if abs(cs - S) <= 1e-12*max(1.0,abs(S)):
       return v / cs? Actually if cs close, return v/cs? To ensure sum S: v*v? We can just return v*(S/cs) if close? But projection should not scale if sum already S? Simpler threshold:
   w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
   cum=np.cumsum(w)
   idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
   theta=(cum-S)/idx
   mask=theta <= w
   if np.all(mask): rho=n else rho=max(np.where(mask)[0])+1
   theta=(cum[rho-1]-S)/rho
   out=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
   return out
```

`perturb_array` modes:
```
def perturb_array(a,n,rng,mode):
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   mean=1.0/n
   if mode==0:
      b=a*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.3,n))
   elif mode==1:
      m=int(rng.integers(1,7))
      phase=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi))
      amp=mean*float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.8))
      b=a + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x/n + phase)
   else:
      m=int(rng.integers(1,7))
      amp=mean*float(rng.uniform(0.1,1.0))
      b=a + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*(x+0.5)/n)
   b=np.where(np.isfinite(b),b,0); b=np.maximum(b,0)
   s=b.sum()
   if s<1e-300: return a.copy()
   return b/s
```
Mode 2 anti sine helps.

def optimize_pool(starts,n,budget_s,rng,target=1.50,max_pool=6,initial_chunk=10.0):
    starts=[normalize_seq(s,n) for s in starts]

       chunk = min(base, deadline-now-5)
       if chunk < 1.5: break

Details of `random_ab`:
```
def random_ab(rng, M, scale=1.0):
    theta=np.zeros(2*int(M), dtype=np.float64)
    if M<=0: return theta
    for m in range(1,M+1):
        amp = scale * float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)) / (m ** 0.25)
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
        theta[2*m-2] = amp * np.cos(phase)
        theta[2*m-1] = amp * np.sin(phase)
    return theta
```
Use index from 0. If want anti sine fixed:
```
def fixed_ab(theta_len):
   M=theta_len//2
   theta=np.zeros(theta_len)
   for m in range(1,M+1):
      amp = 1.0 / (m ** 0.3)
      phase=0.5*np.pi  # sin
      theta[2*m-2]=amp*cos(phase) ~0
      theta[2*m-1]=amp
   return theta
```

`interp_periodic` robust:
```
def interp_periodic(a,n):
    a=np.asarray...; if n<=0...
    m=a.size
    if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if m==n: return normalize_seq(a,n)
    f=a.astype(float)
    f=np.where(np.isfinite(f),f,0.0)
    if f.sum() <= 0: return np.ones(n)/n
    f = f / f.sum() * m  # shape mean1
    xp=np.concatenate((np.arange(m,dtype=float), np.array([m],dtype=float)))
    fp=np.concatenate((f, np.array([f[0]],dtype=float)))
    xn=np.linspace(0.0,m,n,endpoint=False)
    fn=np.interp(xn,xp,fp)
    return normalize_seq(fn,n)
```
`normalize_seq(fn,n)` length n okay.

def next_pow2(m):

    if m <= 1: return 1

Spectral_update top-level now:
```
def spectral_update(a,A,C,c,N,gamma,reduction,floor=0.0):
   n=a.size
   cmax=c.max()
   if cmax<=0: return a,2*n*0
   target=cmax*(1.0-reduction)
   if target<0: target=0
   q=c/cmax
   if floor>0: q=np.maximum(q,floor)
   # if gamma not 1
   c_des = target * np.power(q, gamma)
   Cdes=fft(c_des,N)
   ratio=np.ones(C.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
   absC=np.abs(C)
   maxabs=absC.max()
   if maxabs > 0:
      mask = absC > 1e-25 * maxabs
      ratio[mask]=Cdes[mask]/C[mask]
   mag=np.abs(ratio)
   over = mag > 20.0
   if np.any(over): ratio[over] *= 20.0 / mag[over]
   under=(mag>0.0)&(mag<0.05)
   if np.any(under): ratio[under] *= 0.05 / mag[under]
   Anew = A * np.sqrt(ratio)
   b = np.real(ifft(Anew))[:n]
   b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
   s=b.sum()
   if s<1e-300:
       b=a.copy()
   else:
       b = b / s
   B=fft(b,N); CB=B*B; cb=np.maximum(np.real(ifft(CB))[:2*n-1],0.0)
   sc=2.0*n*cb.max()
   return b, sc
```
Could `target` with reduction 0.15 and q^0.6, c_des max=target c max less than old; if projection sum changes, sc maybe higher; okay.

Need if reduction >1? clamp. In local red_list clamp 0.01 to 0.5.

red2 = min(0.5, red1*2.5 + 0.05)
for (gamma, red, floor) in ((0.6,red1,0.0),(0.4,red2,0.1)):
```

```

Detailed `local_optimize`:
```
def local_optimize(initial, n, time_limit, rng, target=1.50):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0), np.inf
    a = normalize_seq(initial, n)
    N = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    A, C, c = state_from_a(a, N, n)
    cur = score_state(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur
    eta = 0.15
    p = 10.0
    use_thresh = False
    no_acc = 0
    it = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(max(0.5, time_limit))
    L = 2*n - 1
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if cur < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur
            best_a = a.copy()
        if best_sc <= target:
            break
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        cmax = c.max()
        if cmax <= 0:
            break
        q = c / cmax
        if use_thresh:
            W = np.zeros_like(c)
            mask = q > 0.75
            W[mask] = np.power(q[mask], p)
            if W.sum() < 1e-12:
                W = np.power(q, p)
        else:
            W = np.power(q, p)
        Wsum = W.sum()
        if Wsum <= 0 or not np.isfinite(Wsum):
            W = np.ones_like(c) / c.size
        else:
            W = W / Wsum
        FW = fft(W, N)
        g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(FW * np.conj(A)))[:n]
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        gm = g.max()
        accepted = False
        if gm > 1e-300:
            gn = g / gm
            for factor in (1.0, 0.25):
                new_a = mirror_update(a, gn, eta * factor)
                newA = fft(new_a, N)
                newC = newA * newA
                newc = np.maximum(np.real(ifft(newC))[:L], 0.0)
                sc = score_state(newc, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                    a = new_a
                    A = newA
                    C = newC
                    c = newc
                    cur = sc
                    no_acc = 0
                    accepted = True
                    eta = min(3.0, eta * factor * 1.1)
                    break
        if not accepted:
            if it % 3 == 0 or no_acc > 5:
                red1 = 0.05
                if cur > target:
                    red1 = (cur - target) / max(cur, 1e-12)
                    if red1 < 0.02: red1 = 0.02
                    if red1 > 0.3: red1 = 0.3
                red2 = min(0.5, red1 * 2.5 + 0.05)
                best_trial = None
                best_trial_sc = cur
                # maybe also try gamma 0.8
                for gamma in (0.55, 0.35):
                    for red in (red1, red2):
                        floor = 0.0 if gamma > 0.45 else 0.1
                        trial_a, trial_sc = spectral_update(a, A, C, c, N, gamma, red, floor)
                        if np.isfinite(trial_sc) and trial_sc < best_trial_sc:
                            best_trial_sc = trial_sc
                            best_trial = trial_a
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                        break
                if best_trial is not None and best_trial_sc < cur * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                    a = best_trial
                    cur = best_trial_sc
                    A, C, c = state_from_a(a, N, n)
                    no_acc = 0
                    accepted = True
                    eta = max(0.03, eta * 0.7)
        if not accepted:
            no_acc += 1
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.9)
            if p > 2.5:
                p *= 0.97
            elif p < 40.0:
                p *= 1.05
            if no_acc % 7 == 0:
                use_thresh = not use_thresh
            if no_acc > 35:
                mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
                a = perturb_array(a, n, rng, mode)
                A, C, c = state_from_a(a, N, n)
                cur = score_state(c, n)
                eta = 0.15
                p = 10.0
                use_thresh = False
                no_acc = 0
            if cur > best_sc * 1.25 and no_acc > 70:
                a = best_a.copy()
                A, C, c = state_from_a(a, N, n)
                cur = best_sc
                eta = 0.1
                p = 15.0
                no_acc = 0
        if accepted and p > 2.0:
            p *= 0.999
    return best_a, best_sc
```

if cur < best_sc: best_sc=cur; best_a=a.copy()
return best_a,best_sc

Now, `make_start_list`:
```
def make_start_list(n, prev, small_pool, rng, extra_random):
    starts=[]
    if prev is not None:
       starts.append(interp_periodic(prev,n))
    if small_pool:
       for ar in small_pool[:5]:
          starts.append(interp_periodic(ar,n))
    M=10
    starts.append(fourier_ab_sequence(fixed_ab(2*M), n))
    starts.append(fourier_ab_sequence(random_ab(rng, 8, 1.0), n))
    for i in range(extra_random):
       M=12 if i%2==0 else 8
       theta=random_ab(rng,M,1.0)
       starts.append(fourier_ab_sequence(theta,n))
    # smooth noise
    noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
    kernel_size=max(3,n//64)
    kernel=np.ones(kernel_size)/kernel_size
    smooth=np.convolve(noise,kernel,mode='same')
    smooth=(smooth-smooth.min())/max(smooth.max()-smooth.min(),1e-12)
    starts.append(0.2+0.8*smooth)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    starts.append(0.5+0.4*np.sin(2*np.pi*x))
    if len(starts)>14:
       starts=starts[:14]
    return starts
```
Use `np.convolve` noise kernel O(n*k) with k=n/64=128, O(1M) okay. Could use FFT but not needed.

def small_shape_search(n_small, budget_s, prev, rng):
    if budget_s <= 10: return []

    for i in range(18):
       starts.append(fourier_ab_sequence(random_ab(rng, 8 if i%2 else 12, 1.0), n_small))
    # maybe add smooth? no
    pool=optimize_pool(starts, n_small, budget_s, rng, target=1.50, max_pool=5, initial_chunk=None)
    return [item[1] for item in pool]

`optimize_pool`:
```
def optimize_pool(starts, n, budget_s, rng, target=1.50, max_pool=6):
    starts_norm=[]
    for s in starts:
       arr=normalize_seq(s,n)
       if arr.size==n: starts_norm.append(arr)
    if not starts_norm: starts_norm=[np.ones(n)/n]
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(0.0,float(budget_s))
    pool=[]
    nstarts=len(starts_norm)
    base=max(2.0, min(25.0, budget_s / max(1, nstarts)))
    for i in range(nstarts):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now > deadline - 10: break
       chunk=min(base, deadline-now-5)
       if chunk<1.0: break
       a,sc=local_optimize(starts_norm[i], n, chunk, rng, target)
       if np.isfinite(sc):
          pool.append([sc,a])
    if not pool:
       a=normalize_seq(np.ones(n),n); sc=score_seq(a,n); pool.append([sc,a])
    pool.sort(key=by_score_pair)
    if len(pool)>max_pool: pool=pool[:max_pool]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 15:
       pool.sort(key=by_score_pair)
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 30: break
       k=min(3, len(pool))
       for i in range(k):
          now=time.monotonic(); rem=deadline-now
          if rem < 15: break
          chunk=min(45.0, max(5.0, (rem-5)/max(1,k-i)))
          a,sc=local_optimize(pool[i][1], n, chunk, rng, target)
          if np.isfinite(sc):
             pool[i][0]=sc; pool[i][1]=a
       # mutate
       now=time.monotonic(); rem=deadline-now
       if rem > 30 and pool:
          mode=int(rng.integers(0,3))
          mut=perturb_array(pool[0][1], n, rng, mode)
          mchunk=min(12.0, max(2.0, rem*0.04))
          mut,msc=local_optimize(mut,n,mchunk,rng,target)
          if np.isfinite(msc):
             pool.append([msc,mut])
             pool.sort(key=by_score_pair)
             if len(pool)>max_pool:
                pool=pool[:max_pool]
    pool.sort(key=by_score_pair)
    return pool
```
Potential if `base` budget/ nstarts but budget_s huge; base capped 25. For n1 12 starts 1200s base25 ->300s initial. Good. For n=16384, 3 starts 500s base25 ->75 initial. Good. For small 20 starts 150 base7.5. Good.
`local_optimize` may break early if target reached. For small, might not.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2360.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=min(float(budget_s), 2355.0)
    if total_budget < 60: total_budget=max(60,budget_s?)
    deadline=t0+total_budget
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    prev=get_previous_array()
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    # small search
    small_n=1024
    small_budget=max(30.0, min(200.0, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.12))
    small_pool=[]
    if deadline-time.monotonic() > small_budget + 100:
        small_pool=small_shape_search(small_n, small_budget, prev, rng)
    # primary
    n1=8192
    starts1=make_start_list(n1, prev, small_pool, rng, extra_random=5)
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()-40
    if remaining > 120:
        pool1=optimize_pool(starts1,n1,max(120,remaining),rng,target=1.50,max_pool=5)
        for item in pool1: update
    # secondary n2
    if best_a is None:
        best_a=np.ones(n1)/n1; best_sc=score_seq(best_a,n1)
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()-40
    n2=16384
    if remaining > 350:
       starts2=[]
       starts2.append(interp_periodic(best_a,n2))
       if best_a is not None and best_sc < np.inf:
          starts2.append(perturb_array(interp_periodic(best_a,n2),n2,rng,0))
       if len(pool1)>1:
          starts2.append(interp_periodic(pool1[1][1],n2))
       if prev is not None and prev.size == n2:
          starts2.append(normalize_seq(prev,n2))
       # fixed small
       starts2.append(fourier_ab_sequence(fixed_ab(20),n2))
       budget2=min(remaining-60, 1200)
       pool2=optimize_pool(starts2,n2,budget2,rng,target=1.50,max_pool=3)
       update
    # final polish
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()-20
    if remaining > 30 and best_a is not None:
       nbest=len(best_a)
       # if remaining big, local best
       budget_final=min(remaining, 900 if nbest>=16384 else 1500)
       a,sc=local_optimize(best_a,nbest,budget_final,rng,target=1.50)
       update
    # fallback final verification
    final=validate_output(best_a)
    lst=[float(x) for x in final]
    try: ver=evaluate_sequence(lst)
    except Exception: ver=np.inf
    if not finite: fallback uniform
    else update maybe
    # final print
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```

Now, possible issue in `fourier_ab_sequence` using f=1+s*u where s ensures f within [0,20]. But if u all positive, cap max maybe reduces dynamic range; okay.
```
def fourier_ab_sequence(theta,n):
   theta=np.asarray(theta).reshape(-1)
   M=theta.size//2
   if n<=0: return np.zeros(0)
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
   u=np.zeros(n)
   t=2*np.pi*x/n
   for m in range(1,M+1):
       A=theta[2*m-2]; B=theta[2*m-1]
       if A !=0 or B !=0:
           ang=m*t
           u += A*np.cos(ang) + B*np.sin(ang)
   umin=u.min(); umax=u.max()
   if umin==0 and umax==0: return np.ones(n)/n
   scale=1.0
   if umin < 0: scale=min(scale, 0.95/(-umin))
   if umax > 0: scale=min(scale, 19.0/umax)
   if scale <=0 or not finite: return uniform
   f=1.0 + scale*u
   f=np.maximum(f,0.0)
   s=f.sum()
   if s<1e-30: return uniform
   return f/s
```
If scale small, mean f≈1 (u mean near0). Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The method combines:
  - correct spectral flattening of a*a (not |A|^2),
  - subgradient / multiplicative (mirror) descent,
  - direct convolution-domain spectral factorization updates,
  - multi-start population search with resampling from shorter lengths.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft


def by_score_pair(item):
    return item[0]


def time_left(deadline):
    return max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    if S <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    s = v.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cum - S) / idx
    mask = theta <= w
    if not np.any(mask):
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.nonzero(mask)[0])) + 1
    theta = (cum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    return out


def normalize_seq(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != n:
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size < n:
            a = np.resize(a, n)
        else:
            a = a[:n]
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def interp_periodic(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return normalize_seq(a, n)
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    s = f.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = (f / s) * float(m)
    xp = np.concatenate((np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64), np.array([m], dtype=np.float64)))
    fp = np.concatenate((f, np.array([f[0]], dtype=np.float64)))
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, m, n, endpoint=False)
    fn = np.interp(xn, xp, fp)
    return normalize_seq(fn, n)


def state_from_a(a, N, n):
    A = fft(a, N)
    C = A * A
    c = np.real(ifft(C))[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return A, C, c


def score_state(c, n):
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * c.max())


def score_seq(a, n):
    a = normalize_seq(a, n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    _, _, c = state_from_a(a, N, n)
    return score_state(c, n)


def mirror_update(a, g, eta):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    gm = g.max()
    if gm <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    r = g / gm
    r0 = r - r.mean()
    e = eta * r0
    emax = np.max(np.abs(e))
    if emax > 8.0:
        e = e * (8.0 / emax)
    b = a * np.exp(-e)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = b.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    b = b / s
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return a.copy()
    return b


def spectral_update(a, A, C, c, N, gamma, reduction, floor=0.0):
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return a, np.inf
    cmax = c.max()
    if cmax <= 0:
        return a, np.inf
    reduction = float(reduction)
    if reduction < 0.0:
        reduction = 0.0
    if reduction > 0.9:
        reduction = 0.9
    target = cmax * (1.0 - reduction)
    q = c / cmax
    if floor > 0:
        q = np.maximum(q, float(floor))
    c_des = target * np.power(q, float(gamma))
    c_des = np.where(np.isfinite(c_des), c_des, 0.0)
    Cdes = fft(c_des, N)
    ratio = np.ones(C.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
    absC = np.abs(C)
    maxabs = float(absC.max()) if absC.size else 0.0
    if maxabs > 0:
        mask = absC > (1e-25 * maxabs)
        np.divide(Cdes, C, out=ratio, where=mask)
    mag = np.abs(ratio)
    over = mag > 20.0
    if np.any(over):
        ratio[over] *= 20.0 / mag[over]
    under = (mag > 0.0) & (mag < 0.05)
    if np.any(under):
        ratio[under] *= 0.05 / mag[under]
    Anew = A * np.sqrt(ratio)
    Anew = np.where(np.isfinite(Anew), Anew, 0.0)
    b = np.real(ifft(Anew))[:n]
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = b.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        b = a.copy()
    else:
        b = b / s
    B = fft(b, N)
    CB = B * B
    cb = np.maximum(np.real(ifft(CB))[:2 * n - 1], 0.0)
    cb = np.where(np.isfinite(cb), cb, 0.0)
    return b, score_state(cb, n)


def perturb_array(a, n, rng, mode=0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    a = normalize_seq(a, n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    mean = 1.0 / n
    mode = int(mode) % 3
    if mode == 0:
        b = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.30, n))
    elif mode == 1:
        m = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = mean * float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.80))
        b = a + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x / n + ph)
    else:
        m = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
        amp = mean * float(rng.uniform(0.10, 1.00))
        b = a + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * (x + 0.5) / n)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = b.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return b / s


def local_optimize(initial, n, time_limit, rng, target=1.50):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), np.inf
    a = normalize_seq(initial, n)
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A, C, c = state_from_a(a, N, n)
    cur = score_state(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur
    eta = 0.15
    p = 10.0
    use_thresh = False
    no_acc = 0
    it = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.5, float(time_limit))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if cur < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur
            best_a = a.copy()
        if best_sc <= target:
            break
        cmax = c.max()
        if cmax <= 0:
            break
        q = c / cmax

        if use_thresh:
            W = np.zeros_like(c)
            mask = q > 0.75
            W[mask] = np.power(q[mask], p)
            if W.sum() < 1e-12:
                W = np.power(q, p)
        else:
            W = np.power(q, p)
        W = np.where(np.isfinite(W), W, 0.0)
        Wsum = W.sum()
        if Wsum <= 0:
            W = np.ones_like(c) / c.size
        else:
            W = W / Wsum

        FW = fft(W, N)
        g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(FW * np.conj(A)))[:n]
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        gm = g.max()
        accepted = False

        if gm > 1e-300:
            gn = g / gm
            for factor in (1.0, 0.25):
                new_a = mirror_update(a, gn, eta * factor)
                newA = fft(new_a, N)
                newC = newA * newA
                newc = np.maximum(np.real(ifft(newC))[:L], 0.0)
                newc = np.where(np.isfinite(newc), newc, 0.0)
                sc = score_state(newc, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                    a = new_a
                    A = newA
                    C = newC
                    c = newc
                    cur = sc
                    accepted = True
                    no_acc = 0
                    eta = min(3.0, eta * factor * 1.10)
                    break

        if not accepted and (it % 3 == 0 or no_acc > 5):
            red1 = 0.05
            if cur > target:
                red1 = (cur - target) / max(cur, 1e-12)
                if red1 < 0.02:
                    red1 = 0.02
                if red1 > 0.30:
                    red1 = 0.30
            red2 = min(0.50, red1 * 2.5 + 0.05)
            best_trial = None
            best_trial_sc = cur
            params = ((0.55, red1, 0.0), (0.35, red2, 0.10))
            for gg, rr, ff in params:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                tr_a, tr_sc = spectral_update(a, A, C, c, N, gg, rr, ff)
                if np.isfinite(tr_sc) and tr_sc < best_trial_sc:
                    best_trial_sc = tr_sc
                    best_trial = tr_a
            if best_trial is not None and best_trial_sc < cur * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                a = best_trial
                cur = best_trial_sc
                A, C, c = state_from_a(a, N, n)
                accepted = True
                no_acc = 0
                eta = max(0.03, eta * 0.70)

        if not accepted:
            no_acc += 1
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.90)
            if p > 2.5:
                p *= 0.97
            elif p < 40.0:
                p *= 1.05
            if no_acc % 7 == 0:
                use_thresh = (not use_thresh)
            if no_acc > 35:
                mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
                a = perturb_array(a, n, rng, mode)
                A, C, c = state_from_a(a, N, n)
                cur = score_state(c, n)
                eta = 0.15
                p = 10.0
                use_thresh = False
                no_acc = 0
            if cur > best_sc * 1.25 and no_acc > 70:
                a = best_a.copy()
                A, C, c = state_from_a(a, N, n)
                cur = best_sc
                eta = 0.10
                p = 15.0
                no_acc = 0

        if accepted and p > 2.0:
            p *= 0.999

    if cur < best_sc:
        best_sc = cur
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, best_sc


def fourier_ab_sequence(theta, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    M = theta.size // 2
    if M <= 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 2.0 * np.pi * x / float(n)
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        A = float(theta[2 * m - 2])
        B = float(theta[2 * m - 1])
        if A != 0.0 or B != 0.0:
            ang = m * t
            u += A * np.cos(ang) + B * np.sin(ang)
    umin = float(u.min())
    umax = float(u.max())
    if umin == 0.0 and umax == 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    scale = 1.0
    if umin < 0.0:
        scale = min(scale, 0.95 / max(1e-15, -umin))
    if umax > 0.0:
        scale = min(scale, 19.0 / max(1e-15, umax))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = 1.0 + scale * u
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    s = f.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return f / s


def fixed_ab(length):
    theta = np.zeros(int(length), dtype=np.float64)
    M = theta.size // 2
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        amp = 1.0 / (float(m) ** 0.30)
        ph = 0.5 * np.pi
        theta[2 * m - 2] = amp * np.cos(ph)
        theta[2 * m - 1] = amp * np.sin(ph)
    return theta


def random_ab(rng, M, scale=1.0):
    M = int(M)
    theta = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        amp = float(scale) * float(rng.uniform(0.20, 1.00)) / (float(m) ** 0.25)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        theta[2 * m - 2] = amp * np.cos(ph)
        theta[2 * m - 1] = amp * np.sin(ph)
    return theta


def make_start_list(n, prev, small_pool, rng, extra_random):
    starts = []
    if prev is not None:
        starts.append(interp_periodic(prev, n))
    if small_pool:
        for arr in small_pool[:5]:
            starts.append(interp_periodic(arr, n))
    M = 10
    starts.append(fourier_ab_sequence(fixed_ab(2 * M), n))
    starts.append(fourier_ab_sequence(random_ab(rng, 8, 1.0), n))
    for i in range(int(extra_random)):
        Mm = 12 if (i % 2 == 0) else 8
        starts.append(fourier_ab_sequence(random_ab(rng, Mm, 1.0), n))

    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    k = max(3, n // 64)
    kernel = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
    smooth = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode="same")
    d = smooth.max() - smooth.min()
    if d > 0:
        smooth = (smooth - smooth.min()) / d
    else:
        smooth = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    starts.append(0.20 + 0.80 * smooth)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    starts.append(0.50 + 0.40 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x))

    starts = starts[:14]
    return starts


def small_shape_search(n_small, budget_s, prev, rng):
    if budget_s <= 10:
        return []
    starts = []
    if prev is not None:
        starts.append(interp_periodic(prev, n_small))
    M = 8
    starts.append(fourier_ab_sequence(fixed_ab(2 * M), n_small))
    for i in range(18):
        mm = 8 if (i % 2 == 0) else 12
        starts.append(fourier_ab_sequence(random_ab(rng, mm, 1.0), n_small))
    pool = optimize_pool(starts, n_small, budget_s, rng, target=1.50, max_pool=5)
    return [item[1] for item in pool]


def optimize_pool(starts, n, budget_s, rng, target=1.50, max_pool=6):
    starts_norm = []
    for s in starts:
        arr = normalize_seq(s, n)
        starts_norm.append(arr)
    if not starts_norm:
        starts_norm = [np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)]

    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s
    pool = []
    nstarts = len(starts_norm)
    base = max(2.0, min(25.0, budget_s / max(1, nstarts)))

    for i in range(nstarts):
        if time_left(deadline) <= 10:
            break
        chunk = min(base, time_left(deadline) - 5.0)
        if chunk < 1.0:
            break
        a, sc = local_optimize(starts_norm[i], n, chunk, rng, target)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            pool.append([sc, a])

    if not pool:
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        sc = score_seq(a, n)
        pool.append([sc, a])

    pool.sort(key=by_score_pair)
    if len(pool) > max_pool:
        pool = pool[:max_pool]

    while time_left(deadline) > 30:
        pool.sort(key=by_score_pair)
        k = min(3, len(pool))
        for i in range(k):
            rem = time_left(deadline)
            if rem <= 15:
                break
            chunk = min(45.0, max(5.0, (rem - 5.0) / max(1, k - i)))
            a, sc = local_optimize(pool[i][1], n, chunk, rng, target)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                pool[i][0] = sc
                pool[i][1] = a

        rem = time_left(deadline)
        if rem > 30 and pool:
            mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
            mut = perturb_array(pool[0][1], n, rng, mode)
            mchunk = min(12.0, max(2.0, rem * 0.04))
            mut, msc = local_optimize(mut, n, mchunk, rng, target)
            if np.isfinite(msc):
                pool.append([msc, mut])
                pool.sort(key=by_score_pair)
                if len(pool) > max_pool:
                    pool = pool[:max_pool]

    pool.sort(key=by_score_pair)
    return pool


def get_previous_array():
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size < 16:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if arr.sum() <= 0:
        return None
    return arr


def validate_output(a):
    if a is None:
        return np.full(8192, 1.0 / 8192.0, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.full(8192, 1.0 / 8192.0, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
    if arr.sum() < 0.01:
        arr = np.full(8192, 1.0 / 8192.0, dtype=np.float64)
    return arr


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2360.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = min(float(budget_s), 2360.0)
    if total_budget < 60.0:
        total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total_budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    prev = get_previous_array()
    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    print("budget=%.1fs, prev_len=%s" % (total_budget, None if prev is None else prev.size))

    small_pool = []
    small_n = 1024
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    small_budget = max(30.0, min(200.0, rem * 0.12))
    if rem > small_budget + 120:
        small_pool = small_shape_search(small_n, small_budget, prev, rng)
        for item in small_pool:
            sc = score_seq(item, small_n)
            print("small shape score=%.6f" % sc)

    n1 = 8192
    if prev is not None:
        pl = int(prev.size)
        if 4096 <= pl <= 16384:
            n1 = pl
        elif pl > 16384:
            n1 = 16384

    starts1 = make_start_list(n1, prev, small_pool, rng, extra_random=5)
    rem = time_left(deadline) - 40.0
    if rem > 120.0:
        pool1 = optimize_pool(starts1, n1, max(120.0, rem), rng, target=1.50, max_pool=5)
        for item in pool1:
            if item[0] < best_sc:
                best_sc = item[0]
                best_a = item[1].copy()
        print("primary n=%d best=%.6f" % (n1, best_sc))
    else:
        pool1 = []

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.full(n1, 1.0 / n1, dtype=np.float64)
        best_sc = score_seq(best_a, n1)

    n2 = 16384 if n1 <= 8192 else 32768
    rem = time_left(deadline) - 40.0
    if rem > 350.0:
        starts2 = []
        starts2.append(interp_periodic(best_a, n2))
        starts2.append(perturb_array(interp_periodic(best_a, n2), n2, rng, 0))
        if len(pool1) > 1:
            starts2.append(interp_periodic(pool1[1][1], n2))
        if prev is not None and int(prev.size) == n2:
            starts2.append(normalize_seq(prev, n2))
        M = 10
        starts2.append(fourier_ab_sequence(fixed_ab(2 * M), n2))

        budget2 = min(rem - 60.0, 1400.0)
        if budget2 > 60.0:
            pool2 = optimize_pool(starts2, n2, budget2, rng, target=1.50, max_pool=3)
            for item in pool2:
                if item[0] < best_sc:
                    best_sc = item[0]
                    best_a = item[1].copy()
            print("secondary n=%d best=%.6f" % (n2, best_sc))

    rem = time_left(deadline) - 20.0
    if rem > 30.0 and best_a is not None:
        nbest = int(len(best_a))
        final_budget = min(rem, 900.0 if nbest >= 16384 else 1500.0)
        a, sc = local_optimize(best_a, nbest, final_budget, rng, target=1.50)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a
        print("final polish n=%d best=%.6f" % (nbest, best_sc))

    final_a = validate_output(best_a)
    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_a]
    try:
        ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
    except Exception:
        ver = np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(ver):
        final_a = np.full(8192, 1.0 / 8192.0, dtype=np.float64)
        final_list = [float(x) for x in final_a]
        try:
            ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        except Exception:
            ver = 2.0
    else:
        if np.isfinite(ver):
            best_sc = min(best_sc, ver)

    print("FINAL length=%d local_best=%.6f evaluated=%.6f" % (len(final_list), best_sc, ver))
    return final_list
```
